doc_type: module-overview
---

# Paint Template Nodes

`paint_template_nodes/` is the neutral retained-host software-paint family for template-node backed Workbench UI. It replaced the old `host_contract/painter/` directory during the 08 M3.S2 hard cutover. The module still paints editor template nodes in software, but it no longer owns the broad Workbench frame, primitive replay, text, theme, diagnostics, or presenter boundaries.


Current template-node specialized dispatch ownership is folder-backed: `template_nodes/specialized.rs` owns only primary/dropdown/secondary routing order, `specialized/primary.rs` owns the early Material/shell/selection/segmented/button chain, `specialized/dropdown.rs` owns dropdown trigger plus popup-row handoff, and `specialized/secondary.rs` owns the remaining specialized component painter chain.

Current axis-value field style ownership supersedes the older single-file summary above: `template_axis_value_field_style.rs` is now only the style export surface, and `template_axis_value_field_style/{colors,background,border,text}.rs` own the concrete color constants and state-selection rules.

Current viewport-scene architecture primitive ownership supersedes the older single-file summary above: `template_viewport_scene_architecture/primitives.rs` owns only structural helper exports, `primitives/inset_rect.rs` owns shared inset-rect edge emission, and `primitives/alpha.rs` owns alpha-factor color adjustment.

Current viewport-scene floor primitive ownership supersedes the older single-file summary above: `template_viewport_scene_floor/primitives.rs` owns only structural helper exports, `primitives/inset_rect.rs` owns shared inset-rect edge emission, and `primitives/alpha.rs` owns alpha-factor color adjustment.

Current viewport-scene floor grate ownership supersedes the older single-file summary above: `template_viewport_scene_floor/grate.rs` owns only structural exports, `grate/entry.rs` owns repeated grate slot command emission, and `grate/palette.rs` owns grate stripe and edge color constants.

Current shell-panel separator ownership is folder-backed: `template_shell_panels/separators.rs` owns only structural exports; `separators/commands.rs` owns shell-panel kind to separator routing, `geometry.rs` owns pixel-aligned panel rects, `lines.rs` owns only line module wiring, `lines/horizontal.rs` owns top/bottom horizontal line quad emission, `lines/vertical.rs` owns left/right/direct vertical line quad emission, and `metrics.rs` owns drawer-column separator offset constants.

Current popup row ownership is folder-backed: `template_popup_rows.rs` owns only popup row entry dispatch; `menu.rs` owns only menu module wiring and stable exports while `menu/entry.rs` owns menu row count/background iteration plus separator/surface/label/shortcut/adornment command ordering and `menu/style.rs` owns menu item `WorkbenchPopupRowState` projection; `options.rs` owns only option module wiring and stable exports while `options/entry.rs` owns option popup placement, row iteration, surface/label/check-adornment command ordering and `options/style.rs` owns option selected/special marking plus `WorkbenchPopupRowState` projection; `surface.rs` owns only surface module wiring while `surface/background.rs`, `surface/row.rs`, `surface/separator.rs`, and `surface/metrics.rs` own popup background emission, row background/selection-mark emission, separator emission, and popup row order/mark constants respectively; `text.rs` owns only text structural exports while `text/label.rs`, `text/shortcut.rs`, and `text/metrics.rs` own label text commands, shortcut text commands, and shared popup-row text metrics respectively.

Current popup-row test ownership is folder-backed: `template_popup_rows.rs` points test builds at `template_popup_rows_tests/mod.rs`; `adornment.rs` owns menu-item adornment kind, danger, submenu, and icon flag regressions; `style.rs` owns popup row style selector state-priority and runtime state projection regressions; `paint.rs` owns dropdown and menu pixel-output regressions; and `support.rs` owns popup/menu option fixtures plus shared pixel probes.

Current popup row adornment glyph ownership is folder-backed: `template_popup_row_adornments/glyphs.rs` owns only glyph module wiring and the stable `push_popup_row_adornment(...)` export, while `glyphs/dispatch.rs` owns clip gating and adornment-kind fan-out, `symbols.rs` owns only symbol module wiring and stable glyph exports, `symbols/check.rs`, `chevron.rs`, `plus.rs`, and `trash.rs` own their concrete segment layouts, `assets.rs` owns folder/save quad composition, and `segments.rs` owns shared segment-to-command emission.

Current table-row ownership is folder-backed: `template_table_rows.rs` owns only module wiring, test-module attachment, and table-row command entry re-exports; `template_table_rows/commands.rs` owns Workbench table-row command sequencing and generic fallback text sequencing; `identity.rs` owns Table/TableRow recognition; `cells.rs` owns declared/legacy cell extraction, column layout, text commands, and content offsets; `surface.rs` owns row paint rect and surface/separator commands; `actions.rs` owns only action module wiring while `actions/entry.rs` and `actions/geometry.rs` own row action dispatch and action rect placement; `actions/glyphs.rs` owns only glyph module wiring, while `glyphs/gear.rs` and `glyphs/kebab.rs` own gear and kebab glyph command emission respectively; and `style.rs` owns table-row style accessors.

Current table-row test ownership is folder-backed: `template_table_rows.rs` points test builds at `template_table_rows_tests/mod.rs`; `cells.rs` owns declared-option and legacy text parsing regressions, `paint.rs` owns selected/header/tail pixel coverage, `geometry.rs` owns row/content/cell offset regressions, `style.rs` owns shared state-priority regressions, and `support.rs` owns table fixtures plus pixel probes.

Current template-node image ownership is folder-backed: `template_node_images.rs` owns only the structural image entry and re-export surface, while `template_node_images/identity.rs` owns image-source/icon/icon-only recognition, `geometry.rs` owns leading-icon sizing plus image/fitted rect placement, and `command.rs` owns tint/raster lookup plus `HostPaintCommand::image_pixels(...)` emission.

Current template-node dispatch ownership is folder-backed: `template_nodes.rs` owns only module wiring, test entry re-exports, and the template-node command entry re-export; `template_nodes/commands.rs` owns per-node top-level command sequencing, frame-only/opacity gating, specialized dispatch handoff, and generic fallback handoff; `geometry.rs` owns frame translation, visibility, clip, and intersection gating; `ordering.rs` owns z/order and transition opacity; `specialized.rs` owns specialized component painter dispatch and dropdown popup anchoring; `fallback.rs` owns Material/MUI X fallback surface, image, text, and popup-row fallback sequencing.

Current template-node pipeline ownership is folder-backed: `template_node_pipeline.rs` owns only module wiring, public pipeline/test helper re-exports, and the external test-module hook; `template_node_pipeline/clip.rs` owns active damage/template clip intersection; `draw.rs` owns template-node traversal, command collection, profiling counters, and retained command submission; `test_support.rs` owns the test-only RGBA paint helpers.

Current template-node pipeline test ownership is folder-backed: `template_node_pipeline.rs` points test builds at `template_node_pipeline_tests/mod.rs`; `clip.rs` owns active damage/template clip skip and intersection regressions, `dropdown_popup.rs` owns open dropdown row placement plus layout-offset/bounds regressions, `menu_popup.rs` owns popup menu frame pixel coverage, and `support.rs` owns panel/dropdown/menu fixtures plus rect and pixel probes.

Current segmented-control geometry ownership is folder-backed: `template_segmented_control_geometry.rs` owns only structural exports, while `template_segmented_control_geometry/metrics.rs` owns shared segmented/tab font, radius, spacing, and line-height metrics, `segmented.rs` owns only segmented geometry module wiring, `segmented/group.rs` owns group-label geometry, `segmented/body.rs` owns labeled body placement, `segmented/item.rs` owns segment/divider/label rectangles, `segmented/selected.rs` owns selected inset and underline rectangles, and `tabs.rs` owns tab paint, underline, and label rect helpers.

Current segmented-control command ownership is folder-backed: `template_segmented_controls.rs` owns only module wiring, test imports, and the command entry re-export, while `template_segmented_controls/commands.rs` owns segmented-control and Workbench tab dispatch into the concrete child painters. Segmented surface commands are also folder-backed: `template_segmented_controls/segments.rs` owns only structural exports, while `segments/body.rs`, `divider.rs`, and `selected.rs` own group/body command sequencing, segment divider emission, and selected-segment surface/underline emission respectively.

Current segmented-control style ownership is folder-backed: `template_segmented_controls/style.rs` owns only stable style exports, while `style/segmented.rs` owns segmented-control style selection, segmented background/border/text/group-label accessors, selected-segment test accessors, and the idle background token; `style/tabs.rs` owns Workbench tab style selection, tab background test accessor, and tab text color selection.

Current selection-control geometry ownership is folder-backed: `template_selection_control_geometry.rs` owns only structural exports, while `template_selection_control_geometry/metrics.rs` owns checkbox/radio/toggle metric constants, `marks.rs` owns leading mark, label-after-mark, label-gap, and centered-square helpers, `radio.rs` owns radio dot sizing, and `toggle.rs` owns toggle track/thumb rect placement plus declared size fallback helpers.

Current selection-control command ownership is folder-backed: `template_selection_controls.rs` owns only module wiring, test imports, and the command entry re-export, while `template_selection_controls/commands.rs` owns Checkbox/Radio/Toggle dispatch into the concrete control painters.

Current selection-control test ownership is folder-backed: `template_selection_controls.rs` points test builds at `template_selection_controls_tests/mod.rs`; `identity.rs` owns role/control-id classification regressions, `toggle.rs` owns toggle track/thumb geometry and tones, `marks.rs` owns checkbox/radio mark geometry and declared style regressions, `state.rs` owns pressed/loading visual state regressions, `paint.rs` owns pixel-output regressions, and `support.rs` owns node fixtures, resolved-style builders, and pixel probes.

Current selection-control checkbox ownership is folder-backed: `template_selection_controls/checkbox.rs` owns checkbox mark surface, checked-state gating, label placement, and paint-order sequencing, while `template_selection_controls/checkbox/tick.rs` owns checked-tick segment geometry and tick quad emission.

Current selection-control style ownership supersedes the older single-file summary above: `template_selection_controls/style.rs` owns only stable style accessor exports, while `style/checkbox.rs`, `style/radio.rs`, `style/toggle.rs`, and `style/selector.rs` own per-control style accessors and the Workbench selection-control selector handoff.

Current viewport scene root ownership is folder-backed: `template_viewport_scene.rs` owns only the structural export surface and test hook; `template_viewport_scene/commands.rs` owns only viewport scene-kind lookup, pixel-aligned rect gating, and top-level routing into command subowners; `commands/gizmos.rs`, `floor.rs`, `surfaces.rs`, `props.rs`, `architecture.rs`, and `lighting.rs` own concrete child painter routing for their viewport domains; `template_viewport_scene/identity.rs` owns only structural identity exports, `identity/kind.rs` owns `ViewportSceneKind`, `identity/chrome.rs` owns chrome-node exclusion, and `identity/classify.rs` owns only classification module wiring. `classify/entry.rs` owns classification sequencing, `gate.rs` owns prefix/chrome/axis-label exclusion, and `lighting.rs`, `gizmo.rs`, `surfaces.rs`, `floor.rs`, `props.rs`, and `architecture.rs` own their domain-specific WorkbenchViewport control-id mapping. `template_viewport_scene/geometry.rs` owns pixel-aligned scene rect normalization. Viewport scene regression coverage is folder-backed under `template_viewport_scene_tests/`: `architecture.rs`, `floor.rs`, `gizmo.rs`, `light.rs`, `props.rs`, and `surfaces.rs` own domain-specific pixel assertions, while `support.rs` owns styled-node construction, render helpers, pixel probes, and luminance helpers.

Current viewport gizmo ownership is folder-backed: `template_viewport_scene_gizmos.rs` owns only structural exports; `template_viewport_scene_gizmos/axes.rs` owns only axis submodule wiring and stable axis exports, while `axes/colors.rs` owns axis color/glow selection, `axes/cap.rs` owns axis cap geometry and command emission, `axes/line.rs` owns axis line/glow command emission, and `axes/origin.rs` owns axis origin glow plus base-surface command emission. `center.rs` owns gizmo center rod/cube command emission; `selection.rs` owns selection glow command emission.

Current viewport light ownership is folder-backed: `template_viewport_scene_light.rs` owns only the light paint structural entry, while `template_viewport_scene_light/ambient.rs` owns only ambient-light module wiring; `ambient/soft_light.rs`, `ambient/soft_shadow.rs`, and `ambient/floor_reflection.rs` own soft light, soft shadow, and floor reflection command emission respectively; `fixtures.rs` owns wall-light and beacon fixture command emission, and `primitives.rs` owns shared expanded/inset layer emission plus alpha-factor helpers.

Current viewport prop ownership is folder-backed: `template_viewport_scene_props.rs` owns only prop structural exports; `template_viewport_scene_props/cargo.rs` owns only cargo module wiring while `cargo/detail.rs` owns cargo surface highlights and inset shadows and `cargo/inner_frame.rs` owns only inner-frame structural exports. `cargo/inner_frame/entry.rs` owns edge/grid sequencing, `edges.rs` owns cargo inner-frame edge lines, `grid.rs` owns cargo vertical dividers and horizontal grid line emission, and `palette.rs` owns inner-frame line/corner color constants. `property.rs` owns only property module wiring while `property/top.rs`, `property/body.rs`, and `property/palette.rs` own prop top overlays, prop body overlays, and prop overlay palette constants respectively; `rails.rs` owns rack and handrail details; `primitives.rs` owns shared rect-line and radius helpers.

Current viewport floor ownership is folder-backed: `template_viewport_scene_floor.rs` owns only the floor paint structural entry, while `template_viewport_scene_floor/grid.rs` owns glow-backed grid lines, `grate.rs` owns repeated grate slot stripes, `panels.rs` owns floor panel base/inset/shadow command emission, `seams.rs` owns seam glow and core lines, and `primitives.rs` owns shared inset-rect and alpha-factor helpers.

Current material feedback ownership is folder-backed: `template_material_feedback.rs` owns only Material feedback primitive dispatch; `backdrop.rs` owns backdrop recognition and scrim command emission; `progress.rs` owns only progress structural exports, while `progress/entry.rs` owns linear/circular routing and `progress/linear.rs` owns linear progress track/fill/indeterminate bar command emission; `circular_progress.rs` owns only circular-progress structural exports, while `circular_progress/entry.rs` owns image command sequencing, `geometry.rs` owns square image frame placement, `key.rs` owns retained image-key formatting, and `pixels.rs` owns circular progress raster pixel generation; `state.rs` owns Material progress recognition, percent, mode, tone, and color state helpers.

Current notification center ownership is folder-backed: `template_notification_center.rs` owns only module wiring and the notification-center entry re-export; `template_notification_center/commands.rs` owns NotificationCenter popup gating, panel/empty/row command sequencing, and row iteration; `identity.rs` owns center recognition plus header/empty text state; `panel.rs` owns panel surface, header text, and empty-message command emission; `row.rs` owns notification row surface, severity mark, title, and message command emission; `style.rs` owns row state colors and severity colors. Notification-center layout is also folder-backed: `layout.rs` owns only stable layout exports, while `layout/common.rs` owns pixel alignment, `layout/metrics.rs` owns panel/header/row/text metrics, `layout/panel.rs` owns header and empty-message rectangles, and `layout/row.rs` owns row, mark, title, message, and row text-width rectangles.

Current command palette ownership is folder-backed: `template_command_palette.rs` owns only module wiring and the command-palette entry re-export; `template_command_palette/commands.rs` owns CommandPalette popup gating, panel/empty/row command sequencing, and row iteration; `identity.rs` owns command-palette recognition; `layout.rs` owns only layout module wiring while `layout/common.rs`, `layout/metrics.rs`, `layout/panel.rs`, `layout/rows.rs`, and `layout/search.rs` own pixel alignment, radii/font/spacing metrics, empty-message geometry, row/label/selection-mark geometry, and search/search-text geometry respectively; `panel.rs` owns only panel structural exports while `panel/entry.rs`, `panel/surface.rs`, `panel/search.rs`, and `panel/empty.rs` own panel command order, panel surface emission, search field/text emission, and empty-message command emission respectively; `rows.rs` owns only row module wiring while `rows/entry.rs`, `rows/style.rs`, `rows/surface.rs`, and `rows/label.rs` own command row sequencing, popup-row style projection, surface/selection-mark command emission, and label text command emission respectively.

Current dialog ownership is folder-backed: `template_dialogs.rs` owns only structural exports; `template_dialogs/commands.rs` owns Dialog/ConfirmDialog command sequencing and child-owner fan-out; `identity.rs` owns role recognition and popup-open paint state; `layout.rs` owns pixel alignment plus title/body/action/severity mark rectangles; `surface.rs` owns dialog surface and severity mark command emission; `content.rs` owns title/body fallback and text command emission; `actions.rs` owns only action module wiring while `actions/commands.rs`, `actions/labels.rs`, `actions/metrics.rs`, and `actions/text.rs` own confirm/cancel/default action sequencing, action label/width extraction, action layout/font metrics, and action text command emission respectively. Dialog style is also folder-backed: `style.rs` owns only stable style exports, while `style/tokens.rs`, `severity.rs`, `state.rs`, and `variants.rs` own palette constants, severity parsing/mark colors, unavailable/confirm state, and variant token matching respectively; `style/colors.rs` owns only color structural exports, while `colors/actions.rs`, `border.rs`, `surface.rs`, and `text.rs` own dialog action, border, surface, and title/body text color rules respectively.

Current button ownership is folder-backed: `template_buttons.rs` owns only module wiring, test imports, and the button command entry re-export; `template_buttons/commands.rs` owns Workbench button recognition gating, paint-rect validation, kind/opacity orchestration, surface/text ordering, and glyph content child-owner dispatch; `identity.rs` owns recognition, kind selection, key construction, and add-component classification; `geometry.rs` owns paint rects, radius, and pixel alignment; `style.rs` owns style and opacity resolution; `surface.rs` owns button surface command emission; `content.rs` owns only content module wiring while `content/entry.rs`, `content/glyph.rs`, `content/text.rs`, and `content/metrics.rs` own content layout sequencing, glyph mapping/placement/emission, label text emission, and content metrics respectively. Button regression coverage is now folder-backed under `template_buttons_tests/`: `identity.rs` owns recognition exclusions, `paint.rs` owns rendered pixel assertions, `style.rs` owns declared style/state priority assertions, `geometry.rs` owns layout offset assertions, and `support.rs` owns shared fixtures, resolved style builders, frame helpers, and pixel probes. The 2026-06-24 Project Overview screenshot pass extended Workbench button detection to ordinary editor `Button` variants such as `primary` and `secondary`; button label width now uses `paint_text::measure_fallback_text_width` and fontdue advance metrics instead of a character-count estimate, preventing the 128px `Asset Browser` button from wrapping into a clipped second line while keeping centered content layout.

Current button glyph ownership is folder-backed: `template_button_glyphs.rs` owns only structural exports; `template_button_glyphs/identity.rs` owns glyph selection from button key; `metrics.rs` owns glyph icon size; `shapes.rs` owns plus, trash, and chevron shape dispatch; `segments.rs` owns shared 14px segment scaling and quad emission.

Current chip ownership is folder-backed: `template_chips.rs` owns only module wiring and the chip command entry re-export; `template_chips/commands.rs` owns Workbench chip command sequencing, pixel-aligned rect gating, surface/text ordering, and chevron child-owner dispatch; `template_chips/identity.rs` owns Workbench chip recognition and status-chip exclusion; `geometry.rs` owns pixel alignment and label rect geometry; `style.rs` owns chip surface, border, text, and glyph colors plus line metrics; `surface.rs` owns chip surface command emission; `text.rs` owns label fallback, chevron reserve handling, and text command emission.

Current chip test ownership is folder-backed: `template_chips.rs` points test builds at `template_chips_tests/mod.rs`; `identity.rs` owns Workbench/status chip classification regressions, `paint.rs` owns viewport chip surface/border/text/chevron and focus-border pixel regressions, and `support.rs` owns chip fixtures plus pixel probes.

Current chip glyph ownership is folder-backed: `template_chip_glyphs.rs` owns only structural exports; `template_chip_glyphs/identity.rs` owns chevron availability; `metrics.rs` owns chevron reserve, size, and right inset constants; `chevron.rs` owns chevron rect placement and chevron segment dispatch; `segments.rs` owns 12px segment scaling and quad emission.

Current dropdown ownership is folder-backed: `template_dropdowns.rs` owns only module wiring, test imports, `dropdown_paint_rect` re-export, and the dropdown command entry re-export; `template_dropdowns/commands.rs` owns Workbench dropdown command sequencing plus chevron glyph dispatch; `template_dropdowns/identity.rs` owns Workbench dropdown recognition; `geometry.rs` owns paint rect layout-offset application and pixel alignment; `style.rs` owns placeholder-aware dropdown style lookup; `surface.rs` owns dropdown surface command emission; `text.rs` owns label fallback, placeholder detection, chevron reserve handling, and text command emission.

Current dropdown test ownership is folder-backed: `template_dropdowns.rs` points test builds at `template_dropdowns_tests/mod.rs`; `identity.rs` owns Workbench dropdown classification regressions, `paint.rs` owns closed/open/offset/brightness pixel coverage, `geometry.rs` owns half-pixel height alignment, `style.rs` owns declared-style and shared-state priority regressions, and `support.rs` owns dropdown fixtures, popup option fixtures, resolved-style builders, color scaling, and pixel probes.

Current dropdown glyph ownership is folder-backed: `template_dropdown_glyphs.rs` owns only structural exports; `template_dropdown_glyphs/metrics.rs` owns chevron reserve, size, and right inset constants; `chevron.rs` owns chevron rect placement and style-color dispatch; `segments.rs` owns 14px segment scaling and quad emission.

Current status glyph signal ownership is folder-backed: `template_status_glyphs/signals.rs` owns only Ready/Success/Warning/Info dispatch, while `template_status_glyphs/signals/base.rs` owns signal circle fill, `success.rs` owns the success check mark, `info.rs` owns the info mark, and `warning.rs` owns warning triangle and mark command emission.

Current status glyph icon ownership is folder-backed: `template_status_glyphs/icon_glyphs.rs` owns only `StatusIconKind` dispatch, while `template_status_glyphs/icon_glyphs/kind.rs` owns the enum, `snap.rs` owns Snap glyph segments, `world.rs` owns World ring/grid command emission, and `target.rs` owns Target ring/center/crosshair command emission.

Current status-control ownership is folder-backed: `template_status_controls.rs` owns only module wiring, test imports, and the command entry re-export; `template_status_controls/commands.rs` owns status-control kind dispatch into the concrete painters; `identity.rs` owns status ID classification and `StatusControlKind`; `signals.rs` owns only signal structural exports, while `signals/commands.rs` owns status signal icon/text command emission, `signals/metrics.rs` owns normalized status signal mark width projection, and `signals/test_accessors.rs` owns test-only status signal color accessors; `chips.rs` owns status chip surface, text, and chevron command emission; `icons.rs` owns status icon-button surface and glyph command emission.

Current status-control geometry ownership is folder-backed: `template_status_control_geometry.rs` owns only structural exports, while `template_status_control_geometry/common.rs` owns shared offset rects, `metrics.rs` owns status font/radius/line-height metrics, `signals.rs` owns only signal geometry structural exports, `signals/constants.rs` owns signal geometry constants, `signals/icon.rs` owns signal icon and paint-icon rectangles, `signals/text.rs` owns signal text rectangle and test-visible text gap resolution, `chips.rs` owns chip text/chevron rect helpers, and `icons.rs` owns icon-button glyph rect placement.

Current status-control test ownership is folder-backed: `template_status_controls.rs` points test builds at `template_status_controls_tests/mod.rs`; `identity.rs` owns status ID classification regressions, `signals.rs` owns signal pixel/geometry/style regressions, `chips.rs` owns status chip pixel/layout/state regressions, `icons.rs` owns status icon-button pixel/layout/state regressions, and `support.rs` owns status node fixtures plus pixel probes.

Current tree-row ownership is folder-backed: `template_tree_rows.rs` owns only module wiring, test imports, and the command entry re-export; `template_tree_rows/commands.rs` owns tree-row command sequencing and glyph/label/action fan-out; `identity.rs` owns TreeRow recognition; `style.rs` owns shared style/color/state accessors; `surface.rs` owns row surface and indent-guide command emission; `labels.rs` owns label text command emission; `actions.rs` owns eye/kebab/lock action command emission and lock-action visibility.

Current tree-row test ownership is folder-backed: `template_tree_rows.rs` points test builds at `template_tree_rows_tests/mod.rs`; `identity.rs` owns TreeRow role/control-id/component-role classification regressions, `paint.rs` owns selected/nested/collapsed/loading pixel-output regressions, `style.rs` owns shared state-priority regressions, and `support.rs` owns row fixtures plus pixel probes.

Current tree-row geometry ownership is folder-backed: `template_tree_row_geometry.rs` owns only structural exports, while `template_tree_row_geometry/metrics.rs` owns font/radius/guide/action/spacing metrics and line-height calculation, `rows.rs` owns disclosure/icon rects plus indent-guide x placement, `actions.rs` owns right-side action rect placement, and `labels.rs` owns label rect placement.

Current list-row ownership is folder-backed: `template_list_rows.rs` owns only list-row paint sequencing and adornment dispatch, while `template_list_rows/identity.rs` owns ListRow recognition, `style.rs` owns shared list-row style/color accessors, `surface.rs` owns row surface command emission, and `text.rs` owns label layout plus text command emission.

Current list-row test ownership is folder-backed: `template_list_rows.rs` points test builds at `template_list_rows_tests/mod.rs`; `adornment.rs` owns disabled/loading/selected/chevron adornment priority regressions, `paint.rs` owns selected and disabled list-row pixel-output regressions, `style.rs` owns declared surface/text/adornment color and shared state-priority regressions, and `support.rs` owns list-row fixtures plus pixel probes.

Current shell-panel ownership is folder-backed: `template_shell_panels.rs` owns only shell-panel paint sequencing, pixel alignment, and child dispatch; `template_shell_panels/identity.rs` owns Workbench shell-panel control-id recognition and `ShellPanelKind` exposure, including conservative module content-panel suffix matching; `frame.rs` owns shell-panel frame policy for content-panel border/radius selection; `surface.rs` owns chrome style selection, fill command emission, frame application, and separator dispatch; `separators.rs` owns separator pixel alignment plus separator line emission.

Current shell-panel test ownership is folder-backed: `template_shell_panels.rs` points test builds at `template_shell_panels_tests/mod.rs`; `identity.rs` owns container control-id classification regressions, `paint.rs` owns top-toolbar, side-panel, drawer/status-bar, and drawer-column separator pixel regressions, `state.rs` owns chrome selector loading/focused state paint regressions, and `support.rs` owns panel fixtures plus pixel probes.

Current Material state-layer ownership is folder-backed: `material_state_layer.rs` owns only state-layer command sequencing and test-module exposure; `material_state_layer/state.rs` owns overlay color and interaction opacity selection; `material_state_layer/ripple.rs` owns only stable ripple exports; `ripple/commands.rs` owns ripple enablement gating and command emission; `ripple/geometry.rs` owns pressed-origin geometry, clipping, radius, and diameter helpers.

Current Material state-layer test ownership is folder-backed: `material_state_layer.rs` points test builds at `material_state_layer_tests/mod.rs`; `ripple.rs` owns ripple diameter and pressed-origin geometry regressions, `commands.rs` owns state-layer/ripple command sequencing regressions, and `support.rs` owns shared frame fixtures.

Current Material primitive ownership is folder-backed: `material_primitives.rs` owns only structural exports and root-compatible shared re-exports; `material_primitives/dispatch.rs` owns Material primitive child dispatch and text-field surface dispatch; `shared.rs` owns component-variant token matching and first-non-empty helpers. `material_primitives/alert.rs` owns only alert module wiring and the root command entry re-export; `alert/commands.rs` owns Alert root/slot gating and command ordering; `identity.rs`, `geometry.rs`, `surface.rs`, `icon.rs`, `message.rs`, and `action.rs` own alert classification, layout exports, surface quad emission, icon glyph emission, message text emission, and action/close glyph emission respectively. Alert geometry is also folder-backed: `alert/geometry.rs` owns only stable geometry exports, while `geometry/root.rs` owns root pixel alignment, `icon.rs` owns icon/mark frames, `message.rs` owns message left/right/frame placement, `action.rs` owns action width/frame placement, and `metrics.rs` owns alert geometry constants. Alert style is also folder-backed: `alert/style.rs` owns only stable style exports, while `style/palette.rs`, `radius.rs`, `surface.rs`, `text.rs`, `tokens.rs`, and `variants.rs` own severity palette/container colors, radius rules, surface border/fill rules, text/icon/action/cutout colors, MUI token constants, and variant/color-token parsing respectively. `material_primitives/avatar.rs` owns only Avatar module wiring and the root command entry re-export; `avatar/commands.rs`, `identity.rs`, `geometry.rs`, `style.rs`, `image.rs`, `text.rs`, `glyph.rs`, and `mask.rs` own Avatar command ordering, role recognition, structural geometry exports, colors, image/icon raster lookup, fallback text emission, fallback person glyph emission, and rounded image alpha masking respectively. Avatar geometry is also folder-backed: `avatar/geometry.rs` owns only stable geometry exports, while `geometry/frame.rs`, `radius.rs`, `text.rs`, `child.rs`, and `metrics.rs` own Avatar frame placement, corner-radius rules, text frame/font-size placement, fallback child centering, and geometry constants respectively. `material_primitives/chip.rs` owns only Chip module wiring and the root command entry re-export; `chip/commands.rs`, `identity.rs`, `geometry.rs`, `style.rs`, `surface.rs`, `leading.rs`, `text.rs`, and `delete.rs` own Chip command sequencing, role/slot/state classification, chip slot geometry, colors, surface emission, avatar/icon leading glyph emission, label text emission, and delete glyph emission respectively. `material_primitives/divider.rs` owns only Divider module wiring and the root command entry re-export; `divider/commands.rs`, `identity.rs`, `horizontal.rs`, `vertical.rs`, `lines.rs`, `labels.rs`, `geometry.rs`, and `style.rs` own Divider root/orientation dispatch, role recognition, horizontal/vertical sequencing, line emission, label text emission, geometry, and colors respectively. Other concrete Material primitive families remain in their focused child modules.

Current Material primitive avatar command ownership is folder-backed: `avatar/commands.rs` owns only Avatar root gating, frame validation, background/content/border order, and shared color/image lookup; `commands/surface.rs` owns background quad emission and optional border quad emission; `commands/content.rs` owns image, fallback text, icon, and fallback glyph content selection.

Current Material primitive avatar image ownership is folder-backed: `avatar/image.rs` owns only stable image export wiring; `image/pixels.rs` owns preview/media raster lookup and rounded alpha mask application; `image/icon.rs` owns fallback icon raster lookup through the child frame; `image/command.rs` owns retained image-pixels command emission.

Current Material primitive alert action ownership is folder-backed: `material_primitives/alert/action.rs` owns only stable action export wiring, while `action/entry.rs` owns action-frame/color lookup and close-vs-line dispatch, `close.rs` owns close-mark dot emission, `line.rs` owns fallback action-line emission, and `metrics.rs` owns close-dot sizing.

Current Material primitive badge ownership is folder-backed: `material_primitives/badge.rs` owns only module wiring and the stable primitive command export; `badge/commands.rs` owns only structural command exports, while `commands/sequencing.rs`, `root_surface.rs`, `root_label.rs`, and `overlay.rs` own Badge root/slot command ordering, root surface command emission, root label text command emission, and overlay visibility/frame ordering respectively. Badge overlay commands are also folder-backed: `commands/overlay/surface.rs` owns overlay/dot quad emission and `commands/overlay/text.rs` owns overlay text command emission. `identity.rs` and `labels.rs` own Badge root/slot/dot/invisible recognition and root/overlay label extraction. Badge geometry is also folder-backed: `badge/geometry.rs` owns only stable geometry exports, while `geometry/anchor.rs` owns anchor-origin and overlap offset resolution, `metrics.rs` owns badge sizing/text metrics constants, `model.rs` owns `BadgeTextFrame`, `overlay.rs` owns overlay/dot frame, radius, and overlay text frame construction, and `root_text.rs` owns root label text frame construction. Badge style is folder-backed as well: `badge/style.rs` owns only stable style exports, while `style/root.rs` owns root background/border/text color and width rules, `style/overlay.rs` owns overlay background/text/border color and width rules, `style/palette.rs` owns MUI badge color constants, and `style/tokens.rs` owns badge color token resolution.

Current Material primitive timeline ownership is folder-backed: `material_primitives/timeline.rs` owns only Timeline Dot/Connector/Separator dispatch; `timeline/identity.rs` owns role classification and `TimelinePrimitiveKind`, `dot.rs` owns TimelineDot command emission, `connector.rs` owns TimelineConnector command emission and connector width, `geometry.rs` owns centered dot-square geometry, and `style.rs` owns only style module wiring while `style/connector.rs`, `dot.rs`, and `tokens.rs` own connector color fallback, dot outlined/background/border rules, and timeline tone-token palette resolution respectively.

Current Material primitive chip geometry ownership is folder-backed: `chip/geometry.rs` owns only structural exports; `geometry/frame.rs`, `leading.rs`, `delete.rs`, `label.rs`, and `metrics.rs` own chip frame/radius, leading slot structural exports, delete slot geometry, label layout, and chip metric constants respectively. Chip leading geometry is also folder-backed: `geometry/leading/edge.rs`, `frame.rs`, and `margin.rs` own avatar/icon edge sizing, avatar/icon slot frame placement, and leading/negative slot margins respectively.

Current Material primitive chip style ownership is folder-backed: `chip/style.rs` owns only style module wiring; `style/palette.rs` owns only stable palette exports, while `palette/constants.rs`, `lookup.rs`, and `token.rs` own MUI chip palette constants, main-palette lookup, and color-token matching respectively. `background.rs` owns filled/outlined background selection, `foreground.rs` owns disabled/declared/on-dark text color selection, `border.rs` owns border color and width rules, `avatar.rs` owns avatar background color selection, and `delete.rs` owns delete icon color selection.

Current Material primitive chip delete ownership is folder-backed: `chip/delete.rs` owns only stable delete glyph exports; `delete/entry.rs` owns delete icon frame/color lookup plus diagonal point iteration; `delete/dot.rs` owns retained quad emission for each delete dot; `delete/metrics.rs` owns delete stroke and dot-count constants.

Current Material primitive paper ownership is folder-backed: `material_primitives/paper.rs` owns only module wiring and the stable primitive command export; `paper/commands.rs`, `identity.rs`, `geometry.rs`, and `shadow.rs` own Paper command sequencing, Paper role recognition, pixel-aligned paper rects, and elevation shadow layers respectively. `paper/style.rs` owns only style module wiring, while `style/colors.rs`, `overlay.rs`, `shape.rs`, and `state.rs` own background/border rules, dark elevation overlay alpha, radius rules, and outlined/elevation state projection respectively.

Current Material primitive text-field ownership is folder-backed: `material_primitives/text_field.rs` owns only module wiring and the stable surface command export; `text_field/commands.rs` owns only command module wiring and export; `commands/entry.rs`, `surface.rs`, and `underline.rs` own TextField gating/variant dispatch, outlined/filled/standard surface command emission, and underline command emission respectively; `identity.rs`, `geometry.rs`, `style.rs`, and `variant.rs` own TextField recognition, pixel-aligned frame conversion, fill/stroke/radius rules, and outlined/filled/standard variant selection respectively.

Current Material primitive divider geometry ownership is folder-backed: `divider/geometry.rs` owns only structural exports; `geometry/align.rs`, `metrics.rs`, `orientation.rs`, `extents.rs`, `line_frames.rs`, `label_bounds.rs`, and `text_frame.rs` own pixel alignment, divider metrics/font sizing, orientation detection, horizontal/vertical extents, line frames, stable label-bound exports, and label text frames respectively. `geometry/label_bounds/horizontal.rs` owns horizontal label width/left placement, `vertical.rs` owns vertical label height/top placement, and `align.rs` owns text-align classification.

Current Material primitive divider label ownership is folder-backed: `divider/labels.rs` owns only stable label exports; `labels/text.rs` owns divider label fallback text selection; `labels/horizontal.rs` owns horizontal label gating, text-frame handoff, and retained text command emission; `labels/vertical.rs` owns vertical label gating, text-frame handoff, and retained text command emission.

Current generic template-node surface ownership is folder-backed: `template_node_surface.rs` owns only fallback gating and dispatch; `template_node_surface/eligibility.rs` owns frame-only filtering plus surface/border eligibility; `commands.rs` owns elevation shadow, border/surface quad emission, and Material state-layer/ripple dispatch.

Current generic template-node text ownership is folder-backed: `template_node_text.rs` owns only fallback text sequencing; `template_node_text/eligibility.rs` owns painted-row/icon-only skip rules; `geometry.rs` owns inset, leading-icon avoidance, and text rect placement; `metrics.rs` owns font-size and text metric constants; `command.rs` owns retained text command emission.

Current generic template-node label ownership is folder-backed: `template_node_labels.rs` owns only label precedence sequencing and test-module exposure; `template_node_labels/focus.rs` owns focused text input value override; `property.rs` owns PropertyRow label/value composition; `values.rs` owns text-input-aware fallback value ordering.

Current generic template-node label test ownership is folder-backed: `template_node_labels.rs` points test builds at `template_node_labels_tests/mod.rs`; `property.rs` owns PropertyRow label/value visibility regressions, and `support.rs` owns shared label-node fixtures.

Current list-row glyph ownership is folder-backed: `template_list_row_glyphs.rs` owns only list-row adornment dispatch and test-visible re-exports; `template_list_row_glyphs/selection.rs` owns disabled/selected/check/chevron adornment selection; `geometry.rs` owns right-adornment placement; `segments.rs` owns shared segment-to-quad emission; `shapes.rs` owns only shape structural exports; `shapes/check.rs`, `shapes/chevron.rs`, and `shapes/disabled.rs` own check, right-chevron, and disabled-diamond pixel shapes respectively.

Current field ownership is folder-backed: `template_fields.rs` owns only module wiring, test imports, and the field command entry re-export; `template_fields/commands.rs` owns Workbench text-field command sequencing plus stepper and search glyph dispatch; `template_fields/identity.rs` owns Workbench field recognition and stepper classification; `search.rs` owns search-field semantic detection, left magnifier glyph emission, search text inset, and search placeholder classification; `geometry.rs` owns paint rect layout-offset application and pixel alignment; `style.rs` owns inherited opacity and placeholder-aware field style lookup; `surface.rs` owns field surface command emission; `text.rs` owns label fallback, placeholder detection, stepper/search text reserve handling, and text command emission.

Current field test ownership is folder-backed: `template_fields.rs` points test builds at `template_fields_tests/mod.rs`; `identity.rs` owns Workbench field classification regressions, `paint.rs` owns surface/border/text, focus, disabled, and stepper pixel coverage, `geometry.rs` owns half-pixel height alignment, `style.rs` owns declared focus border, opacity, and shared-state priority regressions, and `support.rs` owns field fixtures plus pixel probes.

Current field stepper ownership is folder-backed: `template_field_stepper.rs` owns only structural exports; `template_field_stepper/command.rs` owns stepper divider and glyph command sequencing; `metrics.rs` owns width, divider color, and arrow segment constants; `segments.rs` owns segment scaling plus quad emission.

Current MUI X primitive ownership is folder-backed: `mui_x_primitives.rs` owns only primitive dispatch; `mui_x_primitives/kind.rs` owns TreeView/DataGrid/Picker/Chart/Chat kind selection; `shared.rs` owns shared role matching, quad emission, radius/background helpers, and component-variant checks. MUI X TreeView is folder-backed as well: `mui_x_primitives/tree_view.rs` owns only stable TreeView exports, while `tree_view/identity.rs` owns TreeView role recognition, `rows.rs` owns only stable row exports, `rows/entry.rs` owns surface command emission and row fan-out, `rows/metrics.rs` owns row-count/inset/indent constants, `rows/row.rs` owns row and marker command emission plus row geometry, and `style.rs` owns surface/row/marker color rules. MUI X DataGrid is folder-backed as well: `mui_x_primitives/data_grid.rs` owns only stable DataGrid exports; `data_grid/identity.rs` owns role recognition; `commands.rs` owns draw order and surface/header/row orchestration; `metrics.rs` owns header height, row height, and row-count rules; `surface.rs` owns surface/header command emission; and `rows.rs` owns selected/default row command emission. MUI X Picker is folder-backed as well: `mui_x_primitives/pickers.rs` owns only stable Picker exports, while `pickers/identity.rs`, `commands.rs`, `field.rs`, `popup.rs`, `geometry.rs`, and `metrics.rs` own Date/Time picker role matching, field/popup command sequencing, field surface/icon emission, popup preview emission, picker frame placement, and picker constants respectively. MUI X Chat is also folder-backed: `mui_x_primitives/chat.rs` owns only structural exports, while `chat/identity.rs`, `commands.rs`, `agent.rs`, `composer.rs`, `metrics.rs`, and `style.rs` own chat role classification, AgentChat/Composer dispatch, AgentChat structural export, composer command emission, chat metrics, and chat surface color selection respectively; `agent/commands.rs`, `surface.rs`, `bubbles.rs`, and `streaming.rs` own AgentChat paint order, shell surface, bubble previews, and streaming indicator respectively. MUI X Chart is folder-backed as well: `mui_x_primitives/charts.rs` owns only stable Chart exports; `charts/identity.rs` owns chart kind role matching and `ChartKind`, `surface.rs` owns chart surface/plot command emission plus bar-vs-raster dispatch, `bars.rs` owns bar-chart command emission, and `charts/raster_commands.rs` owns only raster-command structural exports while `raster_commands/commands.rs`, `dimensions.rs`, `identity.rs`, `line.rs`, `sparkline.rs`, `pie.rs`, and `gauge.rs` own raster command sequencing, raster extent clamping, chart-kind resource key names, concrete raster shape command generation, and gauge value normalization.

Current tooltip ownership is folder-backed: `template_tooltips.rs` owns only structural exports; `template_tooltips/commands.rs` owns tooltip command sequencing, style selection, and glyph dispatch; `identity.rs` owns tooltip recognition; `layout.rs` owns bubble rect and pixel alignment; `surface.rs` owns shadow/surface command emission; `text.rs` owns only structural text exports while `text/entry.rs`, `text/title.rs`, `text/body.rs`, and `text/metrics.rs` own tooltip text order, title fallback/emission, body fallback/emission, and shared text metrics respectively.

Current tooltip test ownership is folder-backed: `template_tooltips.rs` points test builds at `template_tooltips_tests/mod.rs`; `paint.rs` owns Workbench tooltip bubble, arrow, border, icon, title, and body pixel regressions, `style.rs` owns shared-state priority regressions, and `support.rs` owns tooltip fixture construction plus pixel probes.

Current tooltip glyph ownership is folder-backed: `template_tooltip_glyphs.rs` owns only structural exports; `template_tooltip_glyphs/metrics.rs` owns arrow/icon size resolution and clamping; `arrows.rs` owns bubble arrow diamond rasterization and command emission; `icons.rs` owns info-icon rect placement, outline, stem, and dot command emission.

Current section-title ownership is folder-backed: `template_section_titles.rs` owns only module wiring and the section-title command entry re-export; `template_section_titles/commands.rs` owns Workbench section-title command sequencing, icon/text ordering, and glyph child-owner dispatch; `template_section_titles/identity.rs` owns section-title control-id recognition; `geometry.rs` owns pixel alignment, icon rects, and label rects; `style.rs` owns section-title text color, declared color, disabled color, and line metrics; `text.rs` owns label lookup, bold overdraw, and text command emission.

Current section-title test ownership is folder-backed: `template_section_titles.rs` points test builds at `template_section_titles_tests/mod.rs`; `identity.rs` owns section-title recognition regressions, `paint.rs` owns bold-label and leading-icon pixel regressions, `style.rs` owns audited title tone, declared title color, and transform-glyph opacity regressions, and `support.rs` owns section-title fixtures plus pixel probes.

Current section-title glyph ownership is folder-backed: `template_section_title_glyphs.rs` owns only structural exports; `template_section_title_glyphs/identity.rs` owns section-title icon-kind selection; `metrics.rs` owns icon size and gap constants; `style.rs` owns section icon colors and transform opacity; `shapes.rs` owns only shape module wiring and the stable icon export, while `shapes/entry.rs`, `cube.rs`, `mesh.rs`, and `transform.rs` own icon color dispatch and cube/mesh/transform segment lists respectively; `segments.rs` owns shared 14px segment scaling and quad emission.

Current sprite-atlas ownership is folder-backed: `sprite_atlas.rs` owns only source-key to atlas-image resolution sequencing; `sprite_atlas/keys.rs` owns retained source-key entry-name normalization; `discovery.rs` owns project/library atlas manifest discovery; `cache.rs` owns atlas manifest cache keys, metadata invalidation, TOML parsing, and validation; `image.rs` owns atlas texture path resolution and RGBA loading; `uv.rs` owns runtime UV conversion.

Current sprite-atlas test ownership is folder-backed: `sprite_atlas.rs` points test builds at `sprite_atlas_tests/mod.rs`; `resolver.rs` owns project-library atlas artifact resolution coverage, and `support.rs` owns unique temporary root construction.

Current visual asset ownership is folder-backed: `visual_assets.rs` owns only the retained-host export surface and test hook; `visual_assets/pixels.rs` owns the shared pixel payload, `target.rs` owns raster target sizing, `tint.rs` owns tint priority and pixel tinting, `keys.rs` owns cache/resource key generation, `retained.rs` owns retained-image pixel conversion, `template.rs` owns template image source/preview fallback, and `asset.rs` owns runtime `UiVisualAssetRef` lookup. Candidate lookup is folder-backed: `visual_assets/candidates.rs` owns only structural exports, while `candidates/query.rs`, `paths.rs`, `variants.rs`, and `resolution.rs` own image/icon/template candidate generation, editor/workspace path resolution plus URI normalization, SVG variant/deduplicated candidate pushing, and first-existing/SVG path checks respectively. Visual asset loading is also folder-backed: `visual_assets/loading.rs` owns only structural exports, while `loading/cache.rs`, `image.rs`, `key.rs`, `missing.rs`, and `pixels.rs` own cache lookup/store, candidate image loading, pixel cache key formatting, missing-icon fallback generation, and candidate pixel loading respectively. SVG support is also folder-backed: `visual_assets/svg.rs` owns only structural exports, while `svg/cache.rs`, `font.rs`, `image.rs`, `parse.rs`, and `pixels.rs` own SVG tree caching/file parsing, system font database/font-needs scanning, intrinsic image rendering, tree parsing, and target-sized pixel rasterization respectively. MUI icon support is also folder-backed: `visual_assets/mui_icons.rs` owns only structural module wiring and exports, while `mui_icons/candidates.rs`, `names.rs`, `parser.rs`, `svg_document.rs`, `rendering.rs`, and `tests.rs` own local MUI module path discovery, module-name normalization, JS path extraction, SVG document assembly, raster/image rendering handoff, and parser/name regressions respectively.

Current slider geometry ownership is folder-backed: `template_slider_geometry.rs` owns only structural exports; `template_slider_geometry/metrics.rs` owns slider dimensions and text metrics; `rects.rs` owns only rect helper structural exports, while `rects/alignment.rs` owns centered and pixel-aligned rectangle helpers, `rects/track.rs` owns slider track placement plus layout-offset/width-delta projection, `rects/value.rs` owns current value rectangle placement, and `rects/range.rs` owns range-min value rectangle placement; `labels.rs` owns label/value/range-min text formatting; `values.rs` owns percent, range-min percent, tick count, fill-span, and thumb-size rules.

Current slider command ownership is folder-backed: `template_sliders.rs` owns only the structural export surface and test hook; `template_sliders/commands.rs` owns only command module wiring and the stable `push_slider_commands` export; `commands/context.rs` owns Workbench slider recognition consumption, pixel-aligned geometry gating, label/value/track rect collection, percent/range-min/tick extraction, and style context construction; `commands/entry.rs` owns the public command entry match over not-slider/consumed/ready states; `commands/sequence.rs` owns only sequence structural exports, while `sequence/entry.rs` owns ready-context fan-out, `sequence/label.rs` owns label command sequencing, `sequence/track.rs` owns track/tick command sequencing, `sequence/thumbs.rs` owns range/current thumb sequencing, and `sequence/values.rs` owns range/current value text sequencing; `identity.rs` remains the focused recognition/style owner, `track.rs` owns only stable track exports while `track/rail.rs` owns track/fill command emission and `track/ticks.rs` owns tick mark command emission, and `thumb.rs` remains the thumb command emitter; `text.rs` owns only text module exports while `text/label.rs`, `range_min.rs`, and `value.rs` own label text, range-min value surface/text, and value surface/text emission respectively.

Current slider test ownership is folder-backed: `template_sliders.rs` points test builds at `template_sliders_tests/mod.rs`; `identity.rs` owns Workbench slider ID/role/variant recognition regressions, `paint.rs` owns track/fill/thumb/value and hover-halo pixel-output regressions, `style.rs` owns shared selector, declared fill/thumb/color, and halo state regressions, `geometry.rs` owns label/value/track proportion, range-min, and tick-count regressions, and `support.rs` owns slider fixtures, style accessors, resolved style builders, and pixel probes.

Current drag-overlay ownership is folder-backed: `template_drag_overlay.rs` owns only overlay gating and command sequencing; `template_drag_overlay/identity.rs` owns DragOverlay recognition; `layout.rs` owns only layout module wiring while `layout/preview.rs`, `layout/indicator.rs`, and `layout/metrics.rs` own preview/icon/text geometry, drop-indicator geometry, and shared preview/icon/text/indicator metrics respectively; `style.rs` owns allowed versus blocked preview colors; `surface.rs` owns preview surface, icon, and drop-indicator quad emission; `text.rs` owns preview label fallback and text command emission.

Current inspector-row kind ownership is folder-backed: `template_inspector_row_kind.rs` owns only structural exports; `template_inspector_row_kind/kind.rs` owns Inspector row/resource enums; `constants.rs` owns public and internal row IDs; `classifier.rs` owns Inspector row-kind classification and property-row recognition; `matching.rs` owns case-insensitive candidate matching; `bool_values.rs` owns boolean parsing and display text.

Current inspector-row geometry ownership is folder-backed: `template_inspector_row_geometry.rs` owns only structural exports; `template_inspector_row_geometry/metrics.rs` owns shared row, label, field, chevron, and shadow-check metrics; `fields.rs` owns field/select/affordance/chevron rectangles; `labels.rs` owns nested label rectangles; `shadows.rs` owns shadow-check rectangle placement and content-offset fallback.

Current inspector-row glyph ownership is folder-backed: `template_inspector_row_glyphs.rs` owns only structural exports; `template_inspector_row_glyphs/swatches.rs` owns material swatch rect/color and command emission; `cubes.rs` owns mesh cube segment lists; `chevrons.rs` owns down-chevron segment selection; `checks.rs` owns check tick segment lists; `segments.rs` owns shared glyph segment command emission.

Current inspector-row paint ownership is folder-backed: `template_inspector_rows.rs` owns row-kind dispatch only; `template_inspector_rows/resource.rs` owns only resource-row module wiring and the stable resource-row export, while `resource/entry.rs`, `count.rs`, and `field.rs` own label/count/field sequencing, material count width/text emission, and resource field/glyph/value/chevron command emission respectively; `disclosure.rs` owns disclosure row command sequencing; `shadow.rs` owns only shadow-row module wiring and stable exports, while `shadow/select.rs` owns shadow select label/field/value/chevron command sequencing and `shadow/check.rs` owns shadow checkbox label/surface/tick command sequencing; `primitives.rs` owns only primitive module wiring while `primitives/labels.rs`, `field.rs`, and `text.rs` own inspector label text, resource field surfaces, and shared text emission respectively; `style.rs` owns inspector-row colors, radius, and font constants.

Current inspector-row test ownership is folder-backed: `template_inspector_rows.rs` points test builds at `template_inspector_rows_tests/mod.rs`; `identity.rs` owns row-kind classification regressions, `resource.rs` owns resource-row pixel and declared-style regressions, `shadow.rs` owns shadow checkbox/select regressions, `geometry.rs` owns nested select geometry regressions, `disclosure.rs` owns disclosure-row pixel coverage, and `support.rs` owns inspector-row fixtures, resolved-style builders, and pixel probes.

Current alert ownership is folder-backed: `template_alerts.rs` owns only module wiring, test imports, and the alert command entry re-export; `template_alerts/commands.rs` owns alert/toast recognition gating, pixel-aligned rect validation, inline-versus-toast dispatch, and child-owner fan-out; `identity.rs` owns Workbench alert/toast recognition and tone parsing; `inline.rs` owns inline alert surface, icon, and text command emission; `toast.rs` owns only Toast structural exports, while `toast/entry.rs` owns Toast style lookup and surface/icon/text/action sequencing, `toast/surface.rs` owns the Toast surface quad, `toast/icon.rs` owns success mark command emission plus status mark sizing, `toast/text.rs` owns label fallback and text emission, and `toast/action.rs` owns UNDO action text plus close mark emission. Alert layout is also folder-backed: `layout.rs` owns only stable layout exports, while `layout/common.rs` owns pixel alignment and centered-rect helpers, `layout/inline.rs` owns inline alert icon/text frames, `layout/toast.rs` owns toast icon/close/action/text frames and action availability, and `layout/metrics.rs` owns alert/toast font, radius, line-height, inset, gap, close, and action constants.

Current alert test ownership is folder-backed: `template_alerts.rs` points test builds at `template_alerts_tests/mod.rs`; `identity.rs` owns Workbench alert/toast classification regressions, `inline.rs` owns inline alert pixel coverage, `toast.rs` owns Toast pixel and declared-style regressions, `style.rs` owns alert/toast state-priority regressions, and `support.rs` owns alert fixtures, pixel probes, and alpha blending helpers.

Current alert glyph ownership is folder-backed: `template_alert_glyphs.rs` owns only structural exports; `template_alert_glyphs/marks.rs` owns only mark structural exports; `marks/dispatch.rs` owns alert-tone mark dispatch and error close-mark fan-out; `marks/palette.rs` owns the shared dark glyph color; `marks/round.rs` owns info/success round-surface and segment overlay emission; `marks/warning.rs` owns warning triangle rows plus warning mark segment emission; `close.rs` owns close-X segment drawing; `segments.rs` owns shared 18px coordinate scaling, `ALERT_ICON_SIZE`, and segment-to-quad emission.

Current property-row ownership is folder-backed: `template_property_rows.rs` owns only structural exports; `template_property_rows/commands.rs` owns PropertyRow dispatch, label/value routing, and scalar-versus-axis value selection; `identity.rs` owns PropertyRow recognition and component property-row IDs; `layout.rs` owns only layout subowner exports, while `layout/labels.rs` owns label width/text rectangles, `layout/values.rs` owns value-area/scalar/value-text rectangles, `layout/axis.rs` owns axis-label/field group rectangles, and `layout/metrics.rs` owns property-row layout constants; `labels.rs` owns label command emission; `fields.rs` owns only field structural exports, while `fields/scalar.rs` owns scalar field text/surface sequencing plus interactive border color, `fields/axis.rs` owns axis label/field/value sequencing, and `fields/surface.rs` owns shared value-field surface quad emission; `text.rs` owns shared property text command construction.

Current property-row test ownership is folder-backed: `template_property_rows.rs` points test builds at `template_property_rows_tests/mod.rs`; `axis.rs` owns axis/value parsing regressions, `component.rs` owns component row identity and label-width routing regressions, and `support.rs` owns component-row fixtures plus frame helpers.

Current axis-label ownership is folder-backed: `template_axis_labels.rs` owns only Transform axis-label dispatch; `template_axis_labels/identity.rs` owns Label/Icon/SvgIcon recognition, transform-axis token mapping, and scale-link classification; `style.rs` owns axis/link colors and font size; `text.rs` owns axis text command emission; `scale_link.rs` owns scale-link glyph geometry and quad emission.

Current axis-label test ownership is folder-backed: `template_axis_labels.rs` points test builds at `template_axis_labels_tests/mod.rs`; `identity.rs` owns Transform axis-label and scale-link classification regressions, `paint.rs` owns scale-link glyph and compact axis-text pixel regressions, `style.rs` owns audited axis/link tone and scale-link origin regressions, and `support.rs` owns label fixtures, frame conversion, and pixel probes.

Current axis-value field ownership is folder-backed: `template_axis_value_fields.rs` owns only Transform axis-value field command sequencing; `template_axis_value_fields/identity.rs` owns Transform axis-value field recognition and text-input/component-role classification; `geometry.rs` owns compact field rect normalization; `surface.rs` owns field surface command emission; `text.rs` owns value fallback and text command emission. Current axis-value field style ownership is also folder-backed: `template_axis_value_field_style.rs` owns only structural exports, while `template_axis_value_field_style/colors.rs` owns palette constants, `background.rs` owns background state selection, `border.rs` owns border color/width rules, and `text.rs` owns disabled/invalid/declared value-color text selection.

Current axis-value field test ownership is folder-backed: `template_axis_value_fields.rs` points test builds at `template_axis_value_fields_tests/mod.rs`; `identity.rs` owns Transform axis-value field classification regressions, `paint.rs` owns compact/focused/disabled pixel-output regressions, `style.rs` owns declared value-color text regressions, and `support.rs` owns axis/label fixtures plus pixel probes.

Current icon-button glyph shape ownership is folder-backed: `template_icon_button_glyph_shapes/mod.rs` owns only module declarations and the shape-dispatch export, while `dispatch.rs` owns only dispatch module wiring and the shape-dispatch export. `dispatch/entry.rs` owns top-level `IconButtonGlyphKind` family grouping; `dispatch/actions.rs`, `dispatch/assets.rs`, `dispatch/chrome.rs`, `dispatch/files.rs`, `dispatch/tools.rs`, and `dispatch/visibility.rs` own family-local fan-out into existing shape owners. `actions.rs` owns only action-glyph structural exports; `actions/plus.rs`, `actions/trash.rs`, and `actions/filter.rs` own plus, trash, and filter segment lists respectively. `visibility.rs` owns only visibility-glyph structural exports; `visibility/eye.rs`, `visibility/eye_off.rs`, and `visibility/lock.rs` own eye, eye-off, and lock segment lists respectively. `files.rs` owns only file-glyph structural exports; `files/document.rs`, `files/folder.rs`, `files/menu.rs`, and `files/save.rs` own file, folder, menu, and save segment lists respectively. `chrome.rs` owns only chrome-glyph structural exports; `chrome/display.rs`, `chrome/navigation.rs`, and `chrome/playback.rs` own grid/sun, chevron/more, and play segment lists respectively. `tools.rs` owns only tool-glyph structural exports; `tools/pointer.rs`, `tools/transform.rs`, and `tools/snap.rs` own cursor, move/rotate/scale, and snap segment lists respectively. `assets.rs` owns only asset-glyph structural exports; `assets/data.rs`, `assets/media.rs`, and `assets/code.rs` own cube/graph, image/audio, and code segment lists respectively. The older single-file `template_icon_button_glyph_shapes.rs` owner has been removed.

Current icon-button glyph-kind ownership is folder-backed: `template_icon_button_glyph_kind.rs` owns only the structural export surface; `template_icon_button_glyph_kind/kind.rs` owns `IconButtonGlyphKind`, while `mapping.rs` owns Workbench icon-button control/icon keyword mapping.

Current icon-button ownership is folder-backed: `template_icon_buttons.rs` owns only module wiring, test imports, and the icon-button command entry re-export; `template_icon_buttons/commands.rs` owns Workbench icon-button command sequencing, surface/glyph ordering, and glyph child-owner dispatch; `template_icon_buttons/identity.rs` owns icon-button recognition plus status-control exclusion; `geometry.rs` owns paint and glyph rectangles; `style.rs` owns Workbench context selection plus style lookup; `surface.rs` owns icon-button surface command emission.

Current icon-button test ownership is folder-backed: `template_icon_buttons.rs` points test builds at `template_icon_buttons_tests/mod.rs`; `identity.rs` owns Workbench icon-button recognition and status-control exclusion regressions, `paint.rs` owns toolbar/rail/panel pixel-output regressions, `geometry.rs` owns declared offset and icon-size layout regressions, `style.rs` owns declared style/color/radius/state-priority regressions, and `support.rs` owns icon-node fixtures, resolved-style builders, frame helpers, and pixel probes.

Current template style ownership is folder-backed: `template_style.rs` owns only structural exports and the external test hook; `template_style/colors.rs` owns only color module wiring while `colors/surface.rs`, `colors/border.rs`, and `colors/text.rs` own generic surface, border, and text color exports respectively; `colors/surface/entry.rs`, `severity.rs`, `interaction.rs`, and `variants.rs` own surface color priority, severity tones, interaction-state colors, and variant fallback colors respectively; `dimensions.rs` owns border width and corner radius resolution; `elevation.rs` owns elevation shadow eligibility and shadow rect geometry; `overlay.rs` owns MUI overlay component-role recognition; `state.rs` owns shared button disabled and interaction-state helpers.

Current template style test ownership is folder-backed: `template_style.rs` points test builds at `template_style_tests/mod.rs`; `state.rs` owns shared button interaction/disabled priority regressions, `surface.rs` owns surface-color priority and declared-background regressions, and `support.rs` owns button fixtures plus resolved background style construction.

Current template style-color ownership is folder-backed: `template_style_color.rs` owns only the structural export surface; `template_style_color/constants.rs` owns MUI tooltip/snackbar/on-dark constants, `roles.rs` owns `UiStyleColor` and material-role resolution, and `buttons.rs` owns typed button container/tone/primary-contained rules.

Current style-selector root ownership is folder-backed: `style_selector/mod.rs` owns only child module declarations and stable re-exports; `style_selector/state.rs` owns template-node to `UiPainterState` projection, including button interaction-state overrides and retained node flags for hover/press/focus/disabled/checked/selected/open/drag/drop/loading state.

Current Workbench button style-selector ownership is folder-backed: `style_selector/workbench_button.rs` owns only structural exports; `workbench_button/model.rs` owns the button kind/style DTOs; `palette.rs` owns exposed and private Workbench button color tokens; `states.rs` owns only state-style dispatch and stable unavailable-state export, while `states/normal.rs`, `states/interactive.rs`, and `states/unavailable.rs` own normal, hover/pressed/focused, and disabled/loading style construction; `selection.rs` owns declared style-color overrides and add-component specialization; `brightness.rs` owns label brightness scaling; and `tests.rs` owns the loading-state regression.

Current Workbench chrome style-selector ownership is folder-backed: `style_selector/workbench_chrome.rs` owns only structural exports and test-visible token re-exports; `workbench_chrome/model.rs` owns chrome kind/style DTOs; `palette.rs` owns shell chrome color tokens; `fill.rs` owns normal and interactive fill selection; `separators.rs` owns separator state color selection; `selection.rs` owns style assembly; and `tests.rs` owns normal, loading, and active-state regressions.

Current Workbench dropdown style-selector ownership is folder-backed: `style_selector/workbench_dropdown.rs` owns only structural exports; `workbench_dropdown/model.rs` owns dropdown style DTOs; `palette.rs` owns exposed and private dropdown tokens; `colors.rs` owns only color module wiring while `colors/surface.rs`, `colors/border.rs`, `colors/content.rs`, and `colors/declared.rs` own surface color rules, border color rules, text/chevron color rules, and declared-color extraction respectively; `brightness.rs` owns brightness scaling; `selection.rs` owns style assembly; `state.rs` owns unavailable-state helpers; and `tests.rs` owns the loading-state regression.

Current Workbench icon-button style-selector ownership is folder-backed: `style_selector/workbench_icon_button.rs` owns only structural exports; `workbench_icon_button/model.rs` owns context/style DTOs; `palette.rs` owns toolbar/rail/panel icon tokens and radius constants; `selection.rs` owns background/border/radius/glyph style selection plus declared style-color and danger-icon classification; `state.rs` owns unavailable-state helpers; and `tests.rs` owns the loading-state regression.

Current Workbench list-row style-selector ownership is folder-backed: `style_selector/workbench_list_row.rs` owns only structural exports; `workbench_list_row/model.rs` owns the list-row style DTO; `surface.rs` owns background, border, and declared background rules; `colors.rs` owns text/adornment color rules and declared color conversion; `selection.rs` owns style assembly; `state.rs` owns unavailable-state helpers; and `tests.rs` owns the loading-state regression.

Current Workbench popup-row style-selector ownership is folder-backed: `style_selector/workbench_popup_row.rs` owns only structural exports; `workbench_popup_row/model.rs` owns row semantic state, painter-state projection, marked-state helper, and style DTOs; `palette.rs` owns danger text; `selection.rs` owns background/selection-mark/text/shortcut/adornment style rules; `state.rs` owns hot/unavailable-state helpers; and `tests.rs` owns semantic-state and loading regressions.

Current Workbench segmented-control style-selector ownership is folder-backed: `style_selector/workbench_segmented_control.rs` owns only structural exports; `workbench_segmented_control/model.rs` owns segmented/tab style DTOs; `palette.rs` owns group/idle color tokens; `control.rs` owns outer background/border rules; `segments.rs` owns selected segment surface/border/underline metrics and colors; `text.rs` owns selected/idle/group label colors and brightness scaling; `state.rs` owns unavailable-state helpers; and `tests.rs` owns loading-state regressions.

Current Workbench selection-control style-selector ownership is folder-backed: `style_selector/workbench_selection_control.rs` owns only structural exports; `workbench_selection_control/model.rs` owns the selection kind/style DTOs; `palette.rs` owns checkbox/radio/toggle color tokens; `selection.rs` owns only selection module wiring while `selection/entry.rs`, `selection/surface.rs`, and `selection/border.rs` own selection-style assembly, surface state selection, and border state selection respectively; `colors.rs` owns thumb/accent/text/label and declared style-color helpers; `state.rs` owns painter-family and hot/unavailable-state helpers; and `tests.rs` owns the loading-state regression.

Current Workbench slider style-selector ownership is folder-backed: `style_selector/workbench_slider.rs` owns only structural exports; `workbench_slider/model.rs` owns slider style DTOs; `palette.rs` owns exposed and private slider tokens; `colors.rs` owns declared-color conversion; `track.rs` owns track/fill/tick colors; `thumb.rs` owns thumb/outline/halo colors; `value.rs` owns value surface and border colors; `text.rs` owns label/value text colors; `state.rs` owns hot/unavailable state helpers; and `tests.rs` owns the loading-state regression.

Current Workbench status-control style-selector ownership is folder-backed: `style_selector/workbench_status_control.rs` owns only structural exports; `workbench_status_control/model.rs` owns status signal/chip/icon-button style DTOs; `palette.rs` owns status color tokens; `signals.rs` owns signal fill/text/mark rules; `chips.rs` owns status chip background/border/text rules; `icons.rs` owns status icon-button background/border/glyph rules; `helpers.rs` owns declared color and unavailable-state helpers; and `tests.rs` owns unavailable-state regressions.

Current Workbench table-row style-selector ownership is folder-backed: `style_selector/workbench_table_row.rs` owns only structural exports; `workbench_table_row/model.rs` owns the row style DTO; `palette.rs` owns row/header/tail/selection/separator/action/text tokens; `identity.rs` owns header/tail classification; `colors.rs` owns only color module wiring; `colors/background.rs` owns row/header/tail/selected/hover background selection; `colors/border.rs` owns focus border and border-width rules; `colors/action.rs` owns action color selection; `colors/declared.rs` owns declared background/value color extraction; `selection.rs` owns style assembly; `state.rs` owns hot/unavailable helpers; `text.rs` owns cell text color resolution; and `tests.rs` owns the loading-state regression.

Current render-command conversion ownership is folder-backed: `render_command_conversion.rs` owns only the conversion export surface; `render_command_conversion/commands.rs` owns only command module wiring. `commands/entry.rs` owns host-command vector assembly, `commands/command.rs` owns runtime command traversal and clip inheritance, `commands/element.rs` owns paint-element visibility plus payload fan-out, and `commands/brush_payload.rs` owns image-brush handoff plus brush quad fallback. `brush.rs` and `image.rs` own focused brush parsing and image-resource routing. `render_command_conversion/style.rs` owns only style conversion exports, while `style/colors.rs` owns runtime foreground fallback and hex color parsing, `style/frame.rs` owns UI-frame to retained-frame conversion, and `style/text.rs` owns text-alignment fallback placement. `render_command_conversion/text.rs` owns only text module wiring, while `text/commands.rs` owns only command module wiring. `text/commands/entry.rs` owns text color fallback, decoration bracketing, and run/shaped/fallback routing; `commands/runs.rs` owns runtime text-run command emission; `commands/shaped.rs` owns shaped-line command emission; `commands/fallback.rs` owns aligned fallback text command emission; and `text/decorations.rs` owns selection/caret/composition/outline decoration quad emission.

Current render-command replay ownership is folder-backed: `render_commands.rs` owns retained command subtree wiring, runtime command conversion handoff, and the test RGBA entry; `render_commands/command.rs` owns only command module wiring; `command/model.rs` owns `HostPaintCommand`, `kind.rs` owns `HostPaintCommandKind`, and `quad.rs`, `text.rs`, `image.rs`, and `group.rs` own the concrete constructors. `render_commands/draw.rs` owns only the stable replay export while `draw/ordering.rs`, `dispatch.rs`, `quad.rs`, `text.rs`, `image.rs`, `border.rs`, and `color.rs` own stable z-ordering, per-kind dispatch, quad replay, text replay, image replay structural exports, border expansion, and opacity application respectively; `draw/image/entry.rs`, `pixels.rs`, and `placeholder.rs` own concrete image replay, opacity-adjusted image pixels, and image placeholder fallback.

Current table-row cell ownership is folder-backed: `template_table_rows/cells.rs` owns only structural exports and test-visible stable helpers; `cells/text.rs` owns declared-option and legacy text cell extraction; `cells/allocation.rs` owns proportional column allocation, minimum widths, drop-priority visibility, and numeric-column alignment; `cells/geometry.rs` owns table-cell rectangles, header/tail content offsets, and declared per-column x offsets after allocation; `cells/commands.rs` owns text command emission, right-aligned numeric text frames, and cell fan-out; `cells/metrics.rs` owns font/inset/action-width/column-ratio/min-width/drop-order metrics.
